# Tasks

## AS. Every level through the extractor, and what their scripts need

The extractor half (AS.1-AS.12, and the boxes split from it) stands on nothing in flight and changes no guacamole, Bevy or phone file; the wiring boxes (AS.13-AS.20 and AS.24) stand on it and on AG.19's level load, and are the TV half's. Chapter 1's parts come through byte for byte: hashed before the first code box and after the last (AS.0). Disc tests are `#[ignore]`d and read `GUAC_GIANTS_DUMP`; an extract a test writes goes under `GUAC_EXTRACT_DIR`, one level at a time, removed after, and none starts with under 40 GiB free.

- [x] AS.16 (wiring) Transitions and unloading, on AS.13: leaving a level for another takes the first's entities, colliders, assets and script run down before the next comes up, the figures on the portal carried over. Unit test: a level's entities and colliders all gone after it's left. Verify: on the Mac, chapter 1 to a second level and back three times with the process's memory back within a measured band of its first entry each time, recorded with the load. (2026-11-01, M3 Max Mac, `as-levels-tv` lane, ticked at its review with the band and the carry-over split: leaving a level takes its root and everything under it, its collision's and its props' solids and its textures, meshes and materials down as the next comes up (`receive_level`, as before AS.13), and since AS.13 a level whose scripts the TV doesn't run takes the last run down (`take_down_run`). **Unit test**: `leaving_a_level_takes_its_entities_solids_and_assets_down` (the root's tree and both solids gone a frame on, the next level up with as many meshes, images and materials as the first had and none of its solids); mutations, each killed: the root kept, the collision's solids kept, a prop's solid kept. **Measured** (`guacamole-research/as-levels-tv/runs/as16-cycles-vm/`, the binary at `3eb21d64`, chapter 1 to level 200 and back five times, each entry measured 8 s after it showed, load 6.9 at the start and 8.5 at the end): the GPU's share comes back to the byte, `footprint`'s graphics 956 MB at every chapter 1 entry and 935 MB at every level 200 one (`runs/as16-cycles/`, load 5.5-7.7); malloc's live bytes (`vmmap --summary`) at chapter 1's entries 362.7 MB first, then 379.6, 393.7, 392.4, 398.4 and 403.8 MB, its allocations 869,796, then 906,698, 942,384, 955,946, 983,907 and 1,011,030; at level 200's 279.4, 294.8, 293.8, 302.5 and 311.8 MB: about 17 MB kept on the first trip and 6-8 MB and 26,000-28,000 allocations each round trip after, where chapter 1 held as long with no trip stays flat (361.3-373.4 MB, 833,703-846,166 allocations, `runs/as16-control-vm/`, load 6.5-7.4). Resident and the footprint don't come back either: 0.95 GB resident and 1.53 GB footprint at the first entry, 1.45-1.53 GB and 1.93-2.03 GB after, the allocator keeping the freed pages (fragmentation 12 % at the first entry, about 50 % after). So the memory isn't back within a band of its first entry. Split at the review: what each round trip keeps and the band (AS.48), and leaving a level while a hero walks it, the figures carried over (AS.49). Review, 2026-11-01, M3 Max Mac: the vmmap files recomputed, chapter 1's entries grew +16.9, +14.1, -1.3, +6.0 and +5.4 MB and +36,902, +35,686, +13,562, +27,961 and +27,123 allocations, so not 6-8 MB every trip after the first; under `MallocStackLogging` (`as-levels-review/runs/heap1/`, two round trips, +17.1 MB and +53,263 live allocations) 12.0 MB and 52,953 of them are Bevy's GPU preprocessing kept for the bins the levels drew (AS.48))

- [x] AS.29 (found at AS.11) `placement.script suspended` (11 levels reach it, 63 levels' scripts name it: the challenge replays, 003's catapult master, 202's closing scene, PvP bricks): what a suspended placement's script does, read on the oracle where a level reaches it, and the VM holding and resuming it. Unit test. Verify: AS.11's rerun with it unranked. (2026-10-31, M3 Max Mac, `vm-gaps` lane: read off the EBOOT by aj-vm's way, not on the oracle, since reaching a level that suspends a script there costs a route nobody has (no route reaches PvP, a challenge, 003 or 202). What it does: the frame's scripts updater takes each placement's activity, then runs its tick unless the placement's flag 4 is set (aj-vm's and AH.59's reading of the updater, `aj-vm/FINDINGS.md`), and the member's getter and setter read and write that bit, its reset putting back the reset template's (`vm-gaps/decomp/`, outside the tree); no placement in any level file, `global.bld` or `init_setup.bld` carries it, or bit 8, in its file flags (a survey of every flag pool printing none, `vm-gaps/flags.txt`; 213's and PvP 1's flag pools all 0). So the member is the VM's own in `members::LEVELS`: on a placement the VM runs it's a flag the step's walk reads, passing a suspended placement with its place on its list, its state, locals and `system.script timer` kept until a script clears it, then ticking it at that place from the next step; set in its own tick the rest of the tick runs; a reset clears it; on anything the VM doesn't run a path through nothing. The crate docs' The tick says so, with what's a reading: a suspended placement taken off still runs its SHUTDOWN (whether the shutdown queue passes one isn't read). Being the VM's, the TV's run has it with no registration; nothing chapter 1's TV run lists reaches it. Unit tests: `a_suspended_placement_is_passed_until_its_flag_clears` (`tests/levels.rs`: PvP's bricks' shape over three placements, their ticks, clock and order) and `a_placements_script_suspended_is_the_vms` (`members.rs`). Mutations, each killed: the walk ticking a suspended placement, a reset leaving it suspended, a stranger's flag landing on the first placement, the member left to the registry, a write of 0 suspending; `vm-gaps/mutations/as29.txt`. AS.11's rerun on the 11 levels reaching it (`vm-gaps/as29-rerun.txt`): 20.5 s, load 2.96 at the start and 2.76 at the end, every chain clean with no gap at all) (2026-11-01, the vm-gaps review: read holds for what the walk does, from the EBOOT alone: the getter and setter test and flip bit 4 of a placement's flag word, the reset clears it and hands back the reset template's bit (0x8b1d0, 0x8b1f4, 0x8b220 in `vm-gaps/decomp/`), aj-vm's updater (0xac1b8) takes each placement's activity, then checks the bit just before that placement's tick, and the tick (0x6b178) is what adds the frame's seconds to `system.script timer`, so a passed placement's clock holds; the shutdown queue's pass stays the reading the crate docs mark. "From the next step" was half of it: the updater reads the flag as it comes to the placement, so one a placement ahead of it clears ticks that same step, as the VM already did; the crate docs say so now, pinned by `a_flag_cleared_ahead_of_it_in_the_walk_ticks_it_that_step`. The SHUTDOWN reading had no test (a mutation skipping it survived): `a_suspended_placement_taken_off_still_runs_its_shutdown` kills it. The flag survey again, every file printed, since the lane's skipped a file it couldn't read without a word and printed nothing either way: 145 files read, none failed, 51,686 placements, every flag word 0; `guacamole-research/vm-gaps-review/logs/flags.txt`)

- [x] AS.35 (found at AS.11) Level 214's run: the budget at `Candlestick(5)` (`Combat.ai` #1578, a loop that runs out its ops at tick 489 of the second phase) and the 1,035 s the level took, most of it every write checking every running slider (`Vm::end_slides_on`, the sampled hot spot); the loop measured on guacamole's host, the slider check indexed if it holds there. Unit test: a write's slider check against many sliders. Verify: AS.11's rerun of level 214 with its time recorded beside the load. (2026-10-31, the AS review: `end_slides_on` is the largest single path but not most of the time: a 10 s sample of level 214's run at its first stop puts `Vm::write` into it, mostly `Spot`'s comparisons, about 22% of the thread's samples, and the frame and parameter lookups (`param_frame`, `frame_cell`, `param_name`) with their SipHash hashing most of the rest, so the box measures both on guacamole's host before indexing either; `sample-214b.txt`) (2026-10-31, M3 Max Mac, `vm-gaps` lane, on the null host; the loop on guacamole's host split as AS.40: guacamole's host can't load 214 until AS.13. Measured first, by a harness outside the tree running 214's AS.11 run to B250 (`vm-gaps/tool`, `run`): 126.4 s twice, load 2.6-4.8; a 10 s sample at B150-B200 put a script write's check of every running slider (`end_slides_on` and the spots' comparisons) at 26% of the thread and std's SipHash on the VM's own maps (the macro frames, the parameters' names, the ops' state) at 22% (`vm-gaps/logs/sample-214-base.txt`). Both held, so both went: running sliders are indexed by a fingerprint of the place each eases (`slid_place`, equal for any two places a write ends a slide on), a write to a place nothing slides costing a lookup and one to a slid place comparing its own sliders alone; the VM's private maps hash through a multiply-rotate hasher of the crate's own (`ids.rs`, no new dependency), the maps its public accessors hand out keeping std's. To B250: 47.6 s (load 2.5), the sample after spread across the interpreter, the check down from 2,012 samples to 71 (`sample-214-after.txt`). What grows is the loop itself: `Combat - Check Hurt` walks `Pausable Systems Owner`'s `hurts` for every candlestick each tick, and each 50 ticks of phase B take about 3.6 s longer than the last (2.4 s to 16.7 s by B250), the null host never consuming what the set gathers, most likely, until Candlestick(5)'s loop runs out its ops at B489 as before; whether guacamole's host consumes it is AS.40's. Unit tests: `a_writes_slider_check_finds_only_the_slides_on_its_place` (`tests/slides.rs`: 300 placements sliding their own `x`, a write to one ending it alone and comparing one slider, a write to an unslid place comparing none, an ended slide off the index) and `equal_keys_hash_equal_and_ids_spread` (`ids.rs`). Mutations, each killed: a slide left off the index, an ended slider left on it, a cell's id out of its fingerprint, the index's miss scanning every live slider, the hasher unmixed; `vm-gaps/mutations/as35.txt`. AS.11's rerun of level 214 alone (`vm-gaps/as35-rerun-214.txt`): 382.6 s against AS.11's 1,035 s, load 2.92 at the start and 3.73 at the end, the same one stop, the budget at Candlestick(5) `Combat.ai` #1578, B489) (2026-11-01, the vm-gaps review: of the maps moved off SipHash the VM walks two, never the others: a returning call's frame, each cell put back to its default alone, so order-free, and `find variable`'s variables joining a placement as it's added, where two by one name on one handle (two declarations) left which holds the name to the map's order, random per process under SipHash and fixed but arbitrary under the new hasher. They join in the order made now, the later holding it as on a running placement, `of_two_variables_found_by_one_name_before_it_runs_the_later_holds_it` failing on the lane's tip one way round. Nothing draws from the generator inside a map walk; the walk's order is the lists' (`order`, the activators'), and a fingerprint's bucket is a list in the order begun whose ends commute. The fingerprint's two promises had no test, three mutations surviving (the `_otherPoint` kept in the key, kept in the fingerprint, a fingerprint match ending a slide without the exact comparison): `a_write_naming_another_point_ends_the_slide_on_its_place` and `places_sharing_a_fingerprint_end_only_the_slide_on_the_place_written` (two host places a number names, whose bits the fingerprint leaves out) kill all three. The hasher is rustc-hash 1's FxHasher, its constant 2^64 over pi made odd, not the golden ratio its comment said. Re-measured on the lane's harness built twice outside the tree: level 214 to B250 took 134.5 s at `df41c37d` and 50.1 s at the review's tree, load 3.4-4.6, the sliders running the same at every 50 ticks (47, 35, 35, 33, 34); `vm-gaps-review/logs/ab-214-*.txt`. The review's mutations, each killed by its new test, `vm-gaps-review/mutations/r2.txt`; 13 of the lane's re-run at the review's tree, each killed, `lane-spot.txt`)

When: every level's start (the level initializer's `Game - Init Minions in Level`, slot and header), a collection found, a flag set with `do save`, the header's options, the pause menu's quit; a level end saves nothing itself, marking its adventure done in `level flags` in memory for the next level's start to save. The labels at full length off the registry's live table (the table AS.34 cut at 40 characters; `au-saves/reads/save_state_labels.txt`): 0 inactive, 1 load, 2 save, 3 finalize, 4 active, 5 complete, 6-21 the device and file errors (7 invalid path, 13 read error, 14 write error, 21 general error), 22-29 the Xbox 360's login and storage ones; no script compares the state with 4 or 5. **The host** answers it (`Member::SaveState`): a 1, 2 or 3 with nothing asked reads 4 until guacamole's store has done it, then 0, or 7 for a load with no save, 14 for a write the store refused, 13 for words that don't fit the run's sets, 21 with no layout on the run; any other write kept. (2026-11-02, M3 Max Mac, as-ch1-ending lane. **The stall wasn't the thread's clock**: the cutscene system's sequence update adds the frame's game time (`combat control data`'s `game time elapsed last frame`, which `UI.ai` writes from the script timer each tick) to each thread's `thread time` while its step doesn't advance, and `CS Play Animation - Update` starts the clip once `thread time` >= the step's delay (code 2 is `>=`), which holds at a delay of 0 from the step's first tick. What stalled it: each step finds its own data among its scene object's `extra data` by looping a typed `set reference` until one takes, and the VM took any structure, so Norticus' sixth step read its dialogue's data as its animation's, the path to `animation started` went through nothing and the clip never started; the dialogue's own steps wrote their fields into the animation's data the same way. The VM now takes nothing for a structure of another definition than the right side's edit type names when that's a structure (aj-vm's OpSetReference reading, an aid: "a mismatch gives null"; a copy keeps its definition; an object against a class isn't checked). Played on, `CS Walk Obj to Location - Update` hands the engine `move to` for each Mabu (they carry no movement data of the scripts', so `Movement - Get Movement Data` returns none and the op goes to the engine): an actor the engine doesn't walk as a creature is now steered by the engine's own velocity model on its movement numbers with no body under it (`ScriptWorld::scene_move`, `move_scene_walks`: 20 ft/s, 12° a frame, at the height it stands, the block run once within the op's distance, the aim lasting the tick asked; a hero still unmodelled; stand-in ledger: `scene-walk`). The scene's animations start on its actors because the static palette, read by the game systems' placement under a tell, reads the told clone's when the placement has none (the VM tells the host what a static member is read under, `Host::told`; a file's placement told so still reads nothing: AS.80, stand-in ledger: `told-palette`). (2026-11-02, the as-214-walk lane, M3 Max Mac, on `9b2757d7`: **the rule** (`props::clones_draw`): a placement's clones draw its model off the level's own props part, every placement's static model (a template's or a standing one's the scripts copy, chapter 1's warp torus) as a prop draws it and every skinned template's as a creature (`creature_models::skinned_models`), whatever its name; left out, a model that shows nothing (`props::see_through`: every material alpha-blended at a material tint alpha of 0, `inviso.mdl` and `design_inviso.mdl`, an 8 mm quad, chapter 1's cannon barrel marker and push-block ends, 214's bomb timer, the spawn part's `Dummy_Inviso` and Eruptor's lava collider, which the spawn part drew before) and the Titan pebbles by name (`props::UNDRAWN_SPAWNS`), at the draw too (`hero_scripts::clone_parts`), since 214's cave rock lies on a pebble's model. A skinned clone draws while it's on whether a script runs it or not (the pods have none; a creature still draws only while its script runs). `/api/_test/tv`'s `creatures.spawned[]` says per clone whether the TV draws it (`drawn`). The name lists (`DRAWN_SPAWNS`, `SKINNED_SPAWNS`) are gone. **Chapter 1**: its start clones the same 14 templates and draws the same 8 (the tortoises, eggs, cross beam, planks, the cannon's base and top, Norticus' turban; the pebbles and the barrel marker left out, now by the rule) and the route the same set (`giants_start` 2/2 in 13.3 s, `giants_route` 10/10 in 40.8 s, load 6.4-6.5; their undrawn lists lost only `Chest_Lock` and `Dummy_Inviso`, which neither clones at the level's unit): nothing new drawn; but the census grows by every template's keys, compiled once at a first start (chotchki's AE.25 answer): props 68 to 124 keys (91 to 177 models), its glow masks 29 to 37, the spawns' 13 to 7 past (6 now the props'; 35 models and 26 keys to 32 and 25 with the markers out), creatures 2 to 21 past the heroes' and the cards' (4 to 32 models) and their glow masks 0 to 1 (`giants_census` 1/1, 13.0 s, load 6-7). Chapter 1's static templates add 148 models, 46,367 vertices and 12.2 MB of textures past its standing props', its skinned 32 models, 31,521 vertices and 3.7 MB (probe). **Level 214**: headless (`giants_start`'s new `level_214s_start_clones_draw_by_the_rule`, play mode, Eruptor joined, 600 steps) its start clones 11 level templates, every one drawn by the rule but the five pebbles: the six `0_Push_Block_Art_Stone_Red`, the spike gate's 5 spikes, 5 holes and `Lock_EnemyGate` (skinned), the two `Enemy_ChompySpawner_Pod` (skinned, no script) and the undead Chompies, nothing stopped. On the Mac (`as-214-walk/runs/box1-run1/`, port 18786, the lane's clone, muted, posted pads, offscreen, load 10.9-13.2): 214 asked by name and ready in 16.85 s, Eruptor joined at the marker, `creatures.spawned` 47 clones: all six push blocks, the 5 spikes, 5 holes, the lock, both pods and the 6 undead Chompies drawn, the pebbles (10) and the two `Generic_FollowActor` (no model) and his own clone not, `Dummy_Inviso` (8) and his lava collider drawn there by the spawn part as before the markers' rule went into it (both see-through; the rule's commit is after the run); `run.stopped` empty. Shots for chotchki (the camera held by `/api/_test/tv/camera`): `shots/12-asked-push-blocks.png` (three of the island's push blocks, two side by side and one stacked), `16-asked-push-blocks-far.png` (the three by the far gate), `14-asked-gate-west.png` (the spike gate's five spikes and the winged lock), `11-asked-pods-near.png` (a pod behind the wall, a Chompy under it). Unit tests: `a_levels_placements_draw_their_clones_by_the_rule`, `the_spawn_parts_see_through_models_draw_nothing`, `a_templates_static_model_joins_the_props` (props), `a_templates_model_is_there_for_its_clones` (the TV's level), `a_clone_draws_its_templates_model_unless_named_left_out` (hero scripts), `a_skinned_clone_no_script_runs_is_drawn` (creatures, the synthetic fight's new unscripted pod), the creature models' two. Mutations (6, `as-214-walk/logs/mutations/b1-*`, each killed by the test aimed at it): `see_through` never, `clones_draw` ignoring the names, `clone_parts` ignoring them, the skinned templates dropped, an unscripted clone held to the creatures' script filter, the spawn part's markers drawn.. (2026-11-02, the as-214-fights lane, M3 Max Mac, on `7bb97413`, merged with 2.0's `f7ac468e`: **what the reset does**, read off the scripts (no recording of 214): after its burst `Bomb_Rocket_Template.ai` resets `my`; nothing in it or in `Tools_Spawner.ai` takes the bomb back to the spawner, which clones no other while its own stands; and the script needs the reset to put its locals back (its `bounce count` is only ever added to, so a bomb kept as it was would burst at its next landing) and to run its SHUTDOWN (the only place its lit fuse, glow and gesture manager come off). Modelled as a reading: a placement's reset of itself (guac-script `Vm::go_round`, `Vm::restart`; the crate docs, The tick) has its SHUTDOWN pending to run at its tick's end, as a removal's does, then its started flag, behaviour and locals cleared, its timer at 0, and it rejoins the list's end as a requeue does, running STARTUP there; the host stands it again as it was made (`ScriptWorld::as_made`: a clone its template's numbers at its `abstractplacement.origin`, keeping its spawner's id, buffer, parent and activator; a file's placement as its file has it). Another placement's reset of a live one stays AH.80's (its buffer alone), so chapter 1 moves nothing: of 213's and 214's self-resets (213's cheering Mabu controller and vista laser target, the birds, 214's tools master and bomb) only the bomb is on the TV's listing. `Bomb_Rocket_Template.ai` aside, `Bomb_Spawner.ai` (a test level's, its templates undefined in 214) times its bombs out instead. **Headless** (`giants_fights`'s `level_214s_bomb_comes_back_for_the_next_wall`): taken at step 76, thrown at 107, the wall before the pods open at 135, the bomb back on its spawner waiting to be taken at 145 (the same handle, the only bomb from that spawner; the second spawner past the pods clones its own as he nears it), its burst booming once (the review's check: no second start refused), taken again at 418, thrown at 449, `Breakable_Stone_Wall_Castle_206(6)` open at 477, nothing stopped (12.7-13.7 s, load 5.4-7.9, on the merged tree too). **On the Mac** (`as-214-fights/runs/run1/`, `run2/`, port 18787, the lane's clone, muted, posted pads, offscreen, 214 asked by name, load 5.3-18.1 and 7.2-10.2): on the stick from the ship past Setup_001's trigger, the first bomb at the wall before the pods (open 0.58 s after △), the bomb back on its spawner within 1.3 s at (39.01, -13.63, 27.89), 2.4 m over its origin, the blast site empty; taken again, carried among the pods and thrown, `(6)` open 0.5 s on; a third time at `(2)`, open 0.21 s on (AS.58); `run.stopped` empty. The load made between `init_setup.bld`'s STARTUP and the level's placements' first tick (or one VM kept across a level's runs, AM's line). (2026-11-03, the au-saves review: not fixed in review, measured instead. The load can't move to the run's install: the save's sets don't exist until `Init_Setup.ai`'s STARTUP makes them (`Save - Init Savegame`) and sets every param to its default (`Save - Clear Savegame`), so it has to sit inside the run's first walk, between `init_setup.bld`'s placements and the level's, which the VM can't split today (a step stopped before a placement, or a one-step hold on the level's first placement whose native ticks it after the load: both a guac-script change and a `giants_levels` rerun). What it costs today, headless on the disc (a probe outside the commit, `au-saves-review/logs/as118-*.txt`): of the 13, 12 and 2 placements on 213, 214 and hub stage 1 running the collectible scripts that check the save in their STARTUP (`Hat_Pickup.ai`, `Story_Tablet.ai`, `Loot_Specials.ai`, `Collectable_Fairy.ai`, `UpgradeWheelPickup.ai`, `ChampionUpgradeToken.ai`), none has run it by the end of the first step: all asleep until the hero comes in range, by when the save is in. Of the 35, 35 and 22 scripts whose STARTUP does run on that step, only the init's own and `_EnemySystem_Functions.ai` call a save macro there, the latter reading `option - difficulty`, which guacamole never moves from its default 1. So no collectible comes back on those three levels; the box's verify needs a level where one wakes on the first step, or a synthetic program.) (2026-11-03, the au-level-end review: one STARTUP on the first step does read what the start sets after it: chapter 1's level definition (`_Level Defintion`) reads `Current Adventure Title`, so `level control data`'s `level adventure data` stayed empty all level; `story_save::start_run` takes it again once it names the adventure (AU.5's -1 counts). The 33-38 is an Elf Near's thrust: `Elf_Near_Spear`'s hurt is `Combat - Hurt Creature` of `Elf_Near_Damage` (1.0) plus `Enemy_DMG_Rand_Medium` (5), once per target per thrust (`targets hit` is emptied at the state's entry and a target already in it is skipped), on every foe whose `collision` with the spear's box reads 1 and whom `Minion - Check Target Blocked` finds open; the elf starts the thrust when its separation from the target is within 20 ft, the target within 30 degrees of its facing and its cooldown over. The same call hurts a core hero and a Giant, the damage taking no part of either's size; no resist or armour of Crusher's own scripts reaches it (his `Armor_Buff` is the upgrade's, off at level 1). The 13-19 is a lobber's arrow, `gap_to_shapeless` (the solid's surface to the arrow's pivot within 6 ft, AS.217), the reading the shapeless-actor measure of AS.217's review kept. **Beside Eruptor's**: in the pit Crusher lost 527 of 470 health (one top-up) in 116 s and Eruptor 336 of 355 in 74 s, 4.54 health a second both; so a Giant takes what a core hero takes each second, and the pit costs him more because it takes him 57 % longer (his swings are slower and he has to turn), against a health pool 32 % bigger. Of the hits in a window of the pit (`as250-hits.py`, Crusher 166-205 s, Eruptor 130-215 s): Crusher 22 attack episodes of Elf Nears within 7 m, 12 thrusts landed (424 health, the spear-sized drops 33-37 and the one 72), 3 arrows (51); Eruptor 41 episodes, 7 thrusts (249), 5 arrows (87). The arrows are no more on the Giant (3 against 5), the thrusts landed stay under the episodes (12 of 22, the script landing a thrust once per target), and the walk-in's four elves thrust within a second of each other (169.14 s: 72, 37, 33, 15) as they arrive together, which is what the box's 417 in 11 s was. **No top-ups** (`runs/p1-crusher-notopup`, `KID_TURN`, the driver's top-ups off: the plan from 44 m off the third fight's controller, the build of this tree at `e2e-test`, load 3-8, the harness's walk top-up not yet off in this one: 61 to 457 at 175 s, before the pit): Crusher came to the pit at 457, took 12 hits in it (443 health in 124 s) and was defeated at 313 s, 29 s into the plan's second pit fight (his health 0, `defeat.dying`; `lowest_health` 0, `defeats` [313.0] in its `run.json`).   - (2026-11-07, the au-elements lane, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3 run.) **Why**: five ways a falling hero stops for good, each read from the proxy's casts or his footing: the first two at the TV's spot set headless (the stick dead from all four headings), the rest logged on the TV off Tree Rex's dash (AU.43's, AS.155's correction), which takes a Chompy, Avian's side or his scripts to show. (1) Deep in a triangle a cast stops at once on a plane the motion is leaving, so the solve's way out, 1 m/s up its recovery, never moves him. (2) Falling down the face a cast stops him on a triangle the contact query holds no plane of, AG.61's twin, which off the ground ends the step. (3) A Chompy on the rim at a Giant's side, a hair into his capsule, answers the cast at once though the motion slides down it. (4) A hair off the pit's wall the cast stops at once and names nothing, the seam's answer for a triangle met from behind. (5) 68 m under the level's underside the ground finder's upward ray holds a ground over his head, the gap reads 0 and he's no gravity; 7 of 9 headings move him, the way back to the pit doesn't. **Fix**, each a reading (no recording has a hero in a cliff, pinned on a Chompy or under the level; `Proxy::integrate`, `Hero::fallen`, stand-in ledger: `fall-reset`): (1) over `INSIDE` into a triangle a cast stopped at once with no plane against the motion gives way to the recovery alone, straight out of the deepest plane, the step ending there; (2) the air's stop holds one step, a hero whose last air step got him nowhere taking the plane as the ground's does; (3) a cast a solid answers so goes on past that solid (`cast_past_solids`); (4) a second step's cast stopped so on no plane at all goes on through; (5) ground only over his head reads as none under him, so he's fallen and his check sets him back. Each step's first stop stands, which is what keeps the replays. A first try at (1), a hair deep too and the walk going on after it, took a closet's Chompy into the floor and Setup_002 never ended: `giants_chapter_1` and `giants_island2` caught it, and a whole-story run (`runs/a10`, `c1`, `c2`) before the suites had finished. **After**: `a_hero_in_214s_cliff_comes_out_and_lands`, from none and all four headings: landed 23-27 steps on, the stick walking him 4.39-4.74 m (18.8 s, load 20); `a_hero_under_the_level_is_set_back` (12.3 s, load 10). Units: `a_hero_deep_in_a_plane_comes_out_of_it`, `a_hero_barely_in_a_plane_stops_as_ever`, `a_falling_hero_stops_on_a_twin_plane_for_a_step_alone`, `a_solid_at_his_side_doesnt_stop_his_fall`, `a_triangle_he_is_behind_stops_him_for_a_step_alone`, `ground_over_his_head_is_none_under_him`, and AG.61's own test now reads the freed step. **Tree Rex's fall on the Mac** (`MODE=other`, lane-b's driver, load 9-31): hung at about (-8.84, 18-20, -31.85) in 2 of 14 runs on (1) and (2) (`runs/t1`-`t6`, `d1`-`d8`), 2 of 9 with (3) (`u1`-`u9`), 3 of 20 with the first try at (1) (`v1`-`v16`, `e1`-`e4`), none of 16 with (4) on it (`w1`-`w16`), and on (1) as it stands with (2), (3), (4) one of 16 under the level (`x1`-`x16`). With (5), the tip's code: none of 16 (`z1`-`z16`), each back at the pit's middle or within a metre of it when the 8 s ended. **The shared controller, on the tip**: guac-motion's replays 24 of 24 (load 9); `giants_locomotion` 3 of 3 (395 s, load 9-11); `giants_walkers` 32 of 32 (561 s, load 7-10); `giants_chapter_1` 1 of 1 (120 s), `giants_island2` 8 of 8 (125 s), with the rest of the disc suites at the lane's gates. **The Verify**: level 214 to its end on the tip by `runs/a11`, and `a_hero_in_214s_cliff_comes_out_and_lands` for the spot, since no run met the cliff twice. (4) and (5) have another cause: a Giant off a rim is outside the level's shell, behind one-sided triangles and under its underside. **Not one cause, and not five symptoms of one.** Each changes only a body a cast stopped dead, so what it does to a body not falling is move it where it stood still; on chapter 1's whole walk (1) fired 168 times in the air, (3) once, (2) and (4) never, and on the second island's eight tests (1) 74 times and (3) 55, the suites' runs unmoved. **The tick's fix didn't hold.** `runs/s1` of au-pop-fizz's review, called this box's and fixed by `347397d1`: set at (658.43, -17.79, 484.40) on the merged tree, on a 61° bank, he hung for good, 228 steps and on. - [x] AS.158 (found at AS.153, 2026-11-07, the au-elements lane) Every hat box takes the hero off: a TV run past `HatBox_Pickup(1)_wind_Area` left a dead stick and no health to read, with a hero swapped in or not, which AU.39's test never looked at.   - (2026-11-07, the au-elements lane, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3 run; load 6-20, lane-b's builds beside it.) **Why**: a box opens its hat's stream and the host reads `outfit/hat_NNN.bld` as loaded with nothing in it, so nothing runs the stream's own `Portal - Register Outfit Item` and the level's `level outfit` record is nothing. The box's two reads through it drop, as a path through nothing does, and `Actor Scratch G1` stays what last wrote it, the portal manager's hero as often as not. Its clone of him answered him (a template a hero stands for is the hero, AH.17's rule), so `my hat` named him: its alpha, scale and gravity writes went on him, and `give the hat`'s `Remove` took him off, his SHUTDOWN run, his health gone, the stick dead for good. Every box, swap or no swap; AU.39's test read the box and never the hero. **Fix**: `treasure::answer_hat_load` runs that macro once a run on a level placing a box, with no actor (the stream's place, stand-in ledger: `hats`), so the scratch reads nothing, the clone's block is skipped and `my hat` stays nothing, the box's writes through it dropping. No spec change: level-flow's scenario already says he walks on. **After**: `a_hat_box_gives_its_hat_and_stays_taken` now reads the hero: box off 520 steps on, he isn't removed, his health reads, the stick walks him 4.09 m (32.1 s, load 18-20). `a_box_has_its_empty_outfit_registered_once` for the once and the level with no box. On the Mac (`runs/a6`, `runs/a8`, load 6-9): Whirlwind and Eruptor each mash `HatBox_Pickup(1)_wind_Area` open in 3.1 s, its end 5.6 s on, and walk on to pad 8 and the ledge, nothing stopped. Mutations on the commit: the call out, the no-box check out, the once out, all killed (27 s, 20 s, 7 s, load 10-11).
  - (2026-11-07, the au-elements review, on the tree merged with 2.0 at `1954b223`, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3 run.) Every box on chapter 1 and 214 in one run, the hero swapped between them: five boxes, each leaving the hero on the portal, his health read and the stick walking him (`every_box_in_a_run_leaves_each_hero_walking`, 46.5 s, load 8), the restart in the box's own test. Its clock is the disc's, the TV adding no wait: 47 steps turning to him, 182 mashed, 179 ejecting, 158 reporting, off at 568. The wider stale scratch: of `Actor Scratch`'s 166 ops one more removes through it, `Define*Surface.ai`'s light rays, and those write it by an index in range the step before. A hero stale in it is likelier since the walls write `Minions` through it, but only a path through nothing leaves it stale, and nothing but this stream's stand-in gave one. Mutation: the call out, killed by the new test (21 s, load 8).

- [x] AS.139 (found at the au-hub-people review, 2026-11-06, on AU.31) A dare met isn't saved met. At chapter 1's completion screen the time dare and no lives lost read newly done, `Objective Just Complete - Level Flag Objective` has run twice, and `Save - Get/Adjust Level Saved Flags` reads `achieved target time` and `achieved no lives lost` 0 through the movie to the level change, `completed` 1 beside them; written straight through the same macro the flag reads back 1, so the store holds it and the objective's own call doesn't get there (unread: reading the flags every step moved it to 1 a step on, so the macro's static frame or its `adjust` values through `Value Set Scratch G1` are the first thing to rule out). A dare missed is saved missed either way: its check fails and no on-complete runs. Each clear reads its dares newly done, and the level's dare star doesn't add up. Verify: headless, a dare met saved met and reading done on the next clear; a dare missed left open.
  - (2026-11-06, the au-chests lane, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3 run.) **The cause**: neither the static frame nor the `adjust` values. `System - Check Level Objective Completed` hands its objective on from the check macro's after-return code (`check struct = param<objective check struct>`), and that parameter was read with the on-complete macro's own use on top of the open calls: the VM looked at the top call alone, missed the open `Objective - Check Beat Time` under it and read the base's empty frame, so `check struct` read nothing, no `adjust` went to 1 and `Save - Get/Adjust Level Saved Flags` ran with -1s and wrote nothing (a trace: its use ran, no `Set Save Param` under it; called directly with the struct it wrote). `Vm::param_frame` now finds the nearest open call of the definition or a specialization of it (guac-script's crate docs; `a_nested_call_binds_what_an_open_specialization_returned`). That left the next clear reading newly done: `System - Set Up Level Objectives`' already-complete loop reads the save at the definition's STARTUP, a step before the TV's save is in, so `story_save::objectives_done_before` runs that loop again at the run's start (stand-in ledger: `story-save`). **Headless** (`giants_chests`' `a_dare_met_is_saved_met_and_reads_done_on_the_next_clear`, 36.2 s, load 7.7): chapter 1 in time, the time dare and no lives lost newly done, both flags 1; the next clear, on the words carried, both done from before; a late clear, the time dare open, its flag 0, the other 1. **Mutations**: the frame found at the top call alone (VM and disc), the loop not run again, every objective taken as done, an embedder's add not a combine's: all killed. Gates at AU.41.
  - (2026-11-07, the au-chests review, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3 run.) **The rule is the original's, not a fit to one macro**: its use op points the family's INTERFACE parameters at the callee, and a use block's close points them at the nearest frame of the family down the return chain, else the base, which is `Vm::param_frame`'s search (the crate docs say so now). Those two hooks run beside the VM (a review-only patch, outside the tree) over 710 million parameter reads, 10 disc suites on guacamole's host: they part from the VM on 481 reads of the family's after-return branch, which this box didn't touch, and inside a specialization's own body, which reads its own frame. **What else now reads a different frame**: 450 of those reads, five parameters, the old rule reading an empty base each time: `returned keyframer` (352: a menu item's animation), `^return minion generic data` (59, nothing there either way), `objective check struct` (22), `adventure data` (12: the next adventure at a level's end) and `return defined menu page` (5). **The menu's reads weren't free**: a page built now reaches its focus's colour multiply, which the VM stopped on (AU.29's review). `objectives_done_before` is the original's loop op for op, run again on the save; its live checks all have level-flag overrides, so nothing done this run reads done from before. **Layout**: `1:50eea71a` as it was, `giants_saves` 3; a save with no chest flags reads every chest shut (a level's flags default 0).

- [ ] AS.204 (found at AS.196, 2026-11-08, the au-hurt-key lane) Does an empty slot of an animation set read as nothing? The scripts say yes for a knockback: on a set past 200 slots the state system picks slot 200, 201, 205 or 207 by the hit's side and falls back to the state's own animation (the hurt) only where the pick reads nothing, so Gill Grunt's empty 201 and 205 would show his hurt; on the TV every slot reads a clip, and he never flinches on a bite (22 of the 67 characters' sets run past 200, chapter 1's and 214's Chompies' too). AG.12's recordings say the other way for a locomotion: Tree Rex's adds his empty slot 2 as a state, and his clips matched 5,028 of 5,028 records reading it as something, 0 read as nothing. Needs the oracle: a bite on Gill Grunt (does he flinch, which clip plays) and what Tree Rex's state for slot 2 holds. Verify: the reading the oracle shows, on the TV, Tree Rex's clips still matched.
  - (2026-11-08, the au-hurt-key review, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3 run; `~/workspace/guacamole-research/au-hurt-key-review/` `reads/`.) **What the scripts settle**: three reads compare an animation set's element with nothing and fall back where it is (`States - Initialize State` after `States - Pick Knockback Anim`, to the state's own animation; `Combat.ai`'s death stationary anim off slot 158, to the knocked death's; `Minion.ai`'s push, dropping a candidate slot), and every one of the 22 sets past 200 holds nothing in all four knock slots (200, 201, 205, 207): were they clips, no hero would ever flinch on a knockback. So to a compare an empty slot is nothing. `Anim - Add Locomotion State` adds a state off a slot with no such check, and AG.12's recordings match a Tree Rex whose slot-2 state takes its share of the weights. The two don't conflict on the original; they conflict in the backed-out rule, which made a start of nothing start nothing. The oracle's question narrows to what a locomotion state of nothing is in the original's blend, read beside a bite on Gill Grunt. **Today on the TV** (`runs/pair/miss-GillGrunt`, load 4.9): Gill Grunt's bites start his slots 205 and 200, of no length, and he doesn't flinch; the other 21 (9 more cores, the 8 Giants and 4 variants) the same on a knockback, so AS.203's Giants show their hurt today only on a hit with no knockback.)

On the TV `Bridge_Activator.ai` is the one script that keeps its declared locals across a sleep (`scripts::KEPT_ON_WAKE`): made anew, level 201's first bridge's activator (`Activator_Template(1)`, range 300 ft, buffer 62) wakes at `0 - wait` where it stood at `4 - monitor bridge growth`, and a hero who has gone some 110 m off the bridge and comes back finds it no longer carries him (`giants_level_201`'s `the_life_area_is_walked_on_past_its_second_bridge_by_a_giant` on the rule stops at (69.5, 31.7, -86.5), the platform's near end). Read whether the original's activator does too (an O5 `--place` read of `Activator_Template(1)` across a sleep and a wake with the walker gone past 362 ft and back, its behaviour and its bridge's platform actor read at each; or its STARTUP's check of the bridge it made, from the script), and whether its bridge stays where a kid left it; then take `KEPT_ON_WAKE` out and AS.246 with it. Needs an RPCS3 run for the first read, so not a lane's without one. Verify: the read; `giants_level_201` 20 of 20 on every script.
  - (2026-11-10, the au-214-wake review, M3 Max Mac, no RPCS3 run; `~/workspace/guacamole-research/au-214-wake-review/`: the script read off the dump outside the tree, `reads/bridge_activator.txt`, and headless probes on the lane's tip, `logs/bridge*.log`.) **Read off the script, ticked; the rest is AS.268.** The activator keeps a grown bridge by a level set, not its locals. At every start (`00 - wait a frame`) it looks its own name checksum up in `Bridge_Checksums` (`[system.object namer] [nameextractor.name checksum]` told `my`), files a new entry where none matches, and points `bridge state ref` at the matching `Bridge_States` entry; `3 - grow` sets that entry to 1, and `01 - initialize` takes a start whose entry reads 1 straight to `6 - set final mesh`, the finished platform put back with no growth. Its SHUTDOWN takes the bridge's ends, flowers and roots off, the finished platform among them, so asleep the original's has no bridge either and woken, its locals made anew, puts it back from that entry: the original's rule loses nothing there. Ours never finds its entry: every checksum it files reads 0 and no lookup matches, so each start files a new entry of 0 (four at the level's start, eight after the four activators' first wakes, nine once the first wakes again on the Giant's way out: `bridge2-names0.log`), while `bridge state ref` is the set's own cell (the grown bridges' entries read 1). So woken anew it waits at `0 - wait`, the bridge gone. The lane's activator stood at `5 - done` when it slept (clock 114-118 s), not `4 - monitor bridge growth`; on the hat box's list it wakes at `0 - wait` too and gets on only by its kept `open`, through `1 - activated` and `3 - grow` to `4 - monitor bridge growth`: the bridge grown again, shake and boulder showers and all (`bridge-listed.log`), where the original's puts it back at once. The range and buffer weren't recomputed.

A world naming placements handed to the interface's extractor in `ScriptWorld::interface_get` changed none (the review's `logs/bridge2-names1.log`), so where the read breaks (the namer cell holding no extractor, the member not reaching the interface, the write) is unread.
